營收走勢圖

研究員/蔡佾璋 EASONTSAI@uni-psg.com

研究部維持南亞(1303)之投資建議為買進，主要考量：(1)南亞 7
月自結稅後 EPS 0.4 元，雖不如 6 月自結稅後 EPS 2.88 元，但因
6 月有包含權益法認列收益不具可比性，而具可比性的 4、5、7 月
自結稅前淨利率分別是 9.4%、13%、15.7%，歸母淨利率為 4.7%、
9.2%、10.4%，獲利顯著持續提升，符合研究部預期。營收方面，
7+8 月營收達成率 70.6%，優於預期，公司展望 9 月營收將優於 8
月，4Q26 的營收將優於 3Q26，整體營運維持正向。(2)應用於 AI
伺服器的 M6-M8 高階大訂單目前正與客戶洽談生產配合及廠區產
線對接等細節，預計將於 4Q26 完成協商並開始有明顯進展，帶動
後續營收與利潤大幅優化，去瓶頸後的 30 萬張 CCL 產能將全數投
入高階產品生產。(3)廠商持續將擴產資源湧入中高階材料，導致
以 PCB 為主軸的玻纖布、銅箔、CCL、ABF 載板等中低階電子材料
依然緊缺，研究部預期南亞電子材料漲價態勢將延續。此外，南亞
積極推動 AI 供應鏈高階材料的開發、認證與銷售，有顯著成效，
2027 年電子相關獲利占比也將提升至 98%，電子材料占比可望達
70-80%，優於研究部原先預估的65-70%。(4)子公司方面，受惠AI
需求旺盛 ， 南電產能有限但需求持續放大 ，ABF 載板漲價效應延續 ，
2026、2027 年業績皆會有明顯提升；南亞科受惠記憶體供需缺口
再擴大，利潤急速成長。(5)近期石化產品 EG 價格大漲，利差顯著
擴大，對獲利明顯幫助，若市況持續好轉，更明顯的獲利貢獻會落
在 4Q26。研究部分別上修南亞 2026 年、2027 年稅後 EPS 至 13.82
元、21.64 元(原估 12.02 元、16.94 元)，分別+2,325%YoY、
+56.6%YoY，年底淨值 69.8 元、91.4 元。研究部認為 2027 年 CSP
業者對整機成本及零組件價格控管趨嚴 ， 加上上游原料新增產能於
2H27 至1H28 陸續開出，電子材料及CCL 業者議價能力會降低，因
此將電子材料目標評價調降至 20 倍，惟考量目前股價隱含電子材
料評價僅約 12 倍，評價極低，故維持南亞買進投資建議。以2027
年電子材料:南亞科:傳產獲利占比 48:50:2，電子與傳產淨值占比
約 4：6 之比例推算，目標價調升至320 元(20xPER*2027 本業電子
材料 EPS + 5xPER*2027 南亞科貢獻稅後 EPS + 1xPBR*2027 傳產
BVPS)。

重點摘要： 
1. 南亞產品線包括在石化、塑膠、纖維與電子類，分佈廣泛

圖 1、南亞 1H26 部門別營收比重 
電子材料
52.7%
化工產品
16.9%
聚酯產品
15.8%
塑膠加工
12.7%
其他
1.9%

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞是全球最大塑膠加工廠，此外，其電子級玻纖絲為全球
第二大 ， 銅箔基板 、 玻纖布為全球第三大 ， 乙二醇EG為全球第
四大，環氧樹脂、丙二酚BPA為全球第五大業者。南亞產能全
球布局，主要生產基地在台灣，有57個廠， 中國有34個， 美國
有8個廠，共99個生產廠。

同時，南亞持有 26.3%的南亞科為全球第四大 DRAM廠，持有
61.4%的子公司南電為 「PCB三雄」 之一 ， 穩居全球前五大IC載
板廠，也是全球前十大PCB製造商。南亞的產品線為台塑集團
中最廣泛，分佈在石化、 纖維 、塑膠加工及電子材料等，1H26
電子材料佔比由 2025年的46.4% 提升到52.7%，化工產品由
21.8%降至16.9%，聚酯產品由16%略降至15.8%，塑膠產品由
13.9%降至12.7%， 其他1.9%。 公司生產的原材料多為集團內部
自用 ， 僅約少部分對外銷售 ， 外售若對公司更有幫助的話 ， 公
司將會提高外售比率。

聚酯產品包括聚酯薄膜、聚酯纖維(如聚酯棉、POY、DTY以及
瓶用酯粒)等 。 電子材料產品包括銅箔 、 銅箔基板(CCL)、 玻纖
絲、玻纖布、環氧樹脂(EPOXY)等；塑膠產品包括膠布、膠皮
與PU合成皮等。銷售地區廣泛，以中國、美國、台灣為主。

2. 子公司南電、南亞科漲價趨勢延續，母公司南亞間接受惠 
由於AI應用需求大增 ，ASIC需求預估在2026年大幅增加 ， 此類
產品全都用ABF載板 ，ABF載板於4Q25起漲 ，1Q26及2Q26皆維持
續漲價節奏。研究部認為接下來ABF廠議價能力轉強，南電報
價得以持續上漲，母公司南亞間接受惠。

南亞科2Q26持續繳出亮眼的成績，GM及OPM分別達到79.5%及
73.7%的水準 ， 不亞於一線大廠 ， 單季稅後EPS 14.66元 。 南亞
科預期3Q26營運及OPM將進一步提升 ， 且短缺情況至4Q26未見
變化 ， 同時預期市場在2028年前不會發生反轉 ， 母公司南亞間
接受惠。

股權變動方面，董事會之前決議處分南電 3%與南亞科1%的股
權 ， 其中 ， 南電的第一個3%已經處分完畢 ， 後來董事會又通過
再處分3%， 大部分已於2Q26底處分完畢 ， 目前沒有新的處分計
畫，兩次重大處分後仍持有南電61%股權。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

2026/3/25南亞科董事會宣布晟碟 （Sandisk） 、 鎧俠 （Kioxia）、
SK Hynix旗下的Solidigm、思科（Cisco）四家公司分三次私
募認購普通股共3.5億股 ， 股權稀釋約11.3%,研究部推算目前
南亞持有南亞科之持股比例約為26.3%。 而南亞持有南亞科的
3,099萬股權處分目前尚未執行，年底前也不一定會處分，預
估執行後持股比例將調整至25.4%。

3. 電子材料營收自1H26的52.7%提升至9月的60%以上

表1、電子材料為南亞獲利主體(營業利益佔比) 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

缺貨造成電子材料持續漲價，2025年初以來最先出現明顯供
給吃緊的玻纖 ， 近期已擴散至銅箔 、 銅箔基板等材料 ， 各材料
缺貨、漲價還會延續一段時間。中低階產品缺貨主因在於高
階化帶來的產能排擠，隨著玻纖、銅箔及基板高階產品製程
精度提升，生產速度下降，不論銅箔、玻纖、基板，要生產1
單位的高階材料，都必須放棄生產很多單位的中低階材料，
導致缺貨更嚴重，此情況將維持未來一段時間。

電子材料持續成為南亞營運轉型核心，產線維持高稼動率，
銅箔基板、玻纖布與玻纖絲產能利用率達滿載，銅箔與環氧
樹脂產能利用率在8-9成之間。8月電子材料營收占比已提升
至59.8%，較7月增加2.8pct， 正式逼近六成 。 電子材料產業到
2028、2029年仍可維持榮景。

業界有開始擴產，但擴產速度本身有限制，且擴產後也還是
會面臨材料的問題，此外，PCB層數大幅增加，將使得供給增
加，幅度仍跟不上需求的增速 。 整體而言 ， 電子材料供需偏緊
與漲價趨勢短期難以扭轉 ， 銅箔基板及高階PCB的浪潮會有望
延續。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

4. 玻纖獲利率最高，獲利貢獻CCL最大，全品項OPM持續提升 
表 2、南亞各產品別 OPM 逐季轉佳 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞產品價格漲幅因客戶依賴度而異，依客戶每個月機動調
整 。 由於中高階產品供不應求 ， 加上同業將產能轉做高階 ， 導
致一般消費性電子用的E-Glass產能吃緊 ， 這對產品報價相當
有利，報價自2025年開始每個月約調漲5-10%。

獲利率方面，現階段電子材料獲利率以玻纖紗/布排行第一、
近期CCL跟載板漲幅度大，獲利率已逐漸接近玻纖，排名升至
第二，再來是銅箔，而環氧樹脂有8成為非電子用途，因此獲
利率較銅箔低些。

獲利金額貢獻方面，雖然目前獲利率最高的是玻纖紗/布，但
CCL營收規模較大，所以獲利貢獻金額超過玻纖。

持續漲價對3Q26、4Q26利潤率是很重要因素，但除了漲價之
外 ， 產能利用率拉高 ， 固定成本不變 ， 因此訂單及出貨量增加
對利潤率也有一定貢獻， 產品組合改變也很重要， 整體來說 ，
電子材料全產品項的OPM皆持續提升當中。

電子材料產品主要包括： 
I.玻纖布 
南亞的玻纖布產能排名為全球第三，佔南亞的營收提升至 6-
7%，但獲利貢獻位居前列。年產能約5.9億米(以一般布計)，
自用比重原為40%-50%， 但為優先確保下游高階CCL供料無虞 ，
外銷比例已調降至30%-40%(自用仍以市價計價，不影響實質
利潤)，稼動率自6月起持續滿載。受全球嚴重缺料帶動，7-9
月玻纖布價格強勁上揚，預期4Q26動能不減。玻纖布/紗獲利
率為電子材料中最高，2Q26 OPM已達30%-40%以上。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  6

日系知名同業大廠為玻纖紗 /布領導廠商，將專注於高階市
場，而Low DK1玻纖布市場供不應求 ， 進而尋找南亞進行合作 。
此日本同業大廠提供玻纖紗，而南亞可提供Low DK1產能，製
成玻纖布後再回送日廠做後段處理。

此外，日本同業大廠也有供應Low DK2玻纖紗給南亞，織成布
後製成自家CCL基板可對外銷售，Low DK2 產品預計2026年內
會量產 ， 屬長期技術互惠合作 。 初期量不大 ， 但3Q26起日東紡
供貨量將明顯增加，足以支撐公司M8以上高階基板需求，對
於公司後續CCL發展，以及高階玻纖數量擴大會有一定幫助。

E-Glass部分 ， 南亞表示各類電子與消費型載板 、PCB層數持續
升級，即使終端消費總量持平，對E-Glass的用料需求依然持
續擴大。E-Glass自2025年底起漲勢確立，3Q26價格仍持續上
漲，公司在9月會議中表示，中國同業於年中報價約2.2美元/
米，4Q26期貨已漲至2.5美元/米 ， 公司預期2027年有機會上看
3美元/米，漲價動能依然非常強勁。南亞E-Glass採大窯爐生
產，與特殊玻纖的坩堝製程不衝突。

同業因原料緊缺或利潤考量放棄中低階訂單，客戶大量轉向
原料掌握度極高的南亞，使南亞在中階與消費型基板 (如M4
等)的稼動率維持滿載 ， 南亞不會放棄E-Glass供應 ， 未來將憑
藉原料自給優勢，承接大量同業外溢轉單，充分享受價量齊
揚帶來的超額利潤。

特殊玻纖紗使用白金坩堝設備，包含坩堝設計、冷卻拉絲等
環節有許多細微變化，會影響產品的介電常數。全球高階豐
田織布機排單已至2028-2029年 ， 南亞因為有超前部署織布機
產能 ， 提早下訂排單 ， 自4Q26起每年均有新機台如期到位加入
生產。透過台灣新港與昆山廠增設坩堝擴產，2026-2028年兩
岸累計將新增達100多個紡位。

高階石英布（Q-Glass）南亞在技術上仍需突破，初期石英紗
會先從外部供應商取得 ， 但因為公司具備生產玻纖紗的經驗 ，
目前還沒放棄，持續積極投入自主研發。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  7

II.銅箔 
南亞的銅箔產能排名全球第二，近期銅箔佔南亞營收已超過
10%。銅箔年產能約為11萬多噸，目前產能利用率滿載，自用
比例約50%。公司計畫在惠州擴建銅箔產線，將增加2.34萬噸
產能 ， 約擴張2成 ， 預計於2028年1月投產 。 台灣彰濱地區也有
銅箔擴產規劃，主要針對PCB應用為主。

南亞的銅箔目前銷量以HVLP1/2為主 ，HVLP3穩定量產 ， 且具備
HVLP 3.5(介於3與4之間的自有規格)及小量HVLP 4生產能力 ，
另外也有一些特定規格之超薄銅箔已進入量產，HVLP 5 則是
持續開發當中，產品線正積極朝向高階發展。

AI伺服器對超低損耗要求嚴格，高階銅箔結晶晶粒需極細，
製程具物理極限使生產速度大幅變慢，投下相同資源，高階
產出噸數遠低於傳統規格，產生嚴重產能排擠效應。

加工費方面，過去多逐季協商、具價格僵固性， 但自7、8月起
開始出現密集且連續調漲 。3Q26大宗銅箔每噸售價約1.7-1.8
萬美元(含銅價約1.4萬)，加工費約3-4千美元/噸。因結構性
生產降速與高階需求不可逆，預期加工費將具備永久性上升
動能，後續極有機會突破4-5千美元/噸。

III.銅箔基板(CCL) 
南亞的銅箔基板 （CCL） 產能排名全球第三 ， 目前CCL兩岸產能
約618萬張/月，台灣 ：中國崑山與惠州約3：7， 目前稼動率滿
載，公司表示1H26 M4 以上基板銷量逾200萬張。台灣樹林與
新港廠區既有設備的小幅改造去瓶頸進度超前，已提前於
4Q26初實現30萬張產能提升 ， 約+5%YoY， 將全數投入M7以上高
階訂單。

CCL佔南亞的營收比重已於 7月達30%左右，M系列佔其中的
2/3(約整體營收20%)，M4佔M系列的營收60%(約CCL營收40%，
約整體營收12%)，其他規格(M6至M8)則是整體營收8%。

### Page 8

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  8

南亞CCL具備M4至M9等級量產能力，其中，M4是出貨量最大產
品，主要供應NVIDIA用於RTX 50 系列顯卡。1H26 M6-M8處於
客戶開發與送樣階段 ， 與多位知名終端客戶與CSP客戶積極洽
談大單，預計4Q26協商將陸續完成，並將迎來明顯放量(合約
會要求客戶提供保障資金)，產品組合升級亦帶動營收、獲利
明顯提升 。M9目前則是已具量產能力並進入客戶認證測試中 ，
數據回饋理想且有極小量試賣，預計大批量產時程將落在  
1H27。

CCL自4Q25起CCL每月維持3-5%漲幅，7、8月部分客戶補漲達
10-15%，4Q26將延續逐月調漲步調 。 南亞不放棄利潤依然可觀
且客戶轉單持續湧入的M4，採 「高中低階全線掌握」 ，藉由產
品組合優化大幅推升CCL獲利率 （2Q26 OPM近20%，3Q、4Q26將
進一步追趕目前玻纖的40% OPM水準）。

儘管目前台光電在M9市佔率最高，然南亞的策略是透過開發
M10等高階產品來實現「彎道超車」，一舉超越競爭對手。南
亞的M10採用自製配方，即K2特殊玻纖布搭配HVP4銅箔，以及
自研的碳氫樹脂，不需仰賴昂貴且進口申請繁瑣的石英布方
案，在未來的成本控制與產能擴張上，將具備極佳的競爭優
勢。M10目前則是還在認證中，實測Dk/Df數據已達M10理想水
準且優於同業 ， 因測試需時1-2千小時 。 整體認證持續推進中 ，
主控權在客戶端 ， 希望1H27取得更具體的驗證成果 ， 若認證順
利通過的話，2H27將逐漸放量。

5. 預估3Q26稅後EPS 4.28元，+942.7%YoY 
受惠於AI發展帶動中高階電子材料供應緊缺、價量齊揚，且
中東戰事帶動化工、聚酯、塑膠加工等產品售價隨原料行情
飆升 ， 南亞2Q26營收合計836.5億元 ，+21.9%QoQ，+27.3%YoY，
營收達成率97%， 符合預期 。 營業利益113.6億元 ，+203.5%QoQ，
+772%YoY， 主要受惠於AI產業擴大投資 ， 相關材料需求持續成
長，使電路板等電子材料產品利潤提高。而權益法投資(台塑
化、南亞科、台朔重工、麥寮汽電、台塑烯烴(美國))共認列
獲利190.9億元(原估145億元)， 優於預期 。2Q26歸屬母公司淨
利267.5億元(原估183.2億元)， 獲利達成率146%， 優於預期 ，
稅後EPS 3.37元(原估2.31元)，+87.7%QoQ，YoY虧轉盈。

展望2H26，雖預期3Q26聚酯部門因紡織用途市況不佳 ， 營收看
季減 ， 化工部門營收看持平 ， 然而 ， 塑膠加工部門營收穩健季
增，且公司看好AI算力規模於未來幾年內仍會大幅成長，邊
緣運算將帶動終端電子材料全系列產品升級，高階材料銷售
比重逐漸提高，電子材料將帶動南亞整體營收將持續向上攀
升，預期9月營運表現將優於8月，3Q26優於2Q26，4Q26優於
3Q26。。研究部預估3Q26營收924.3億元(原估877.9億元)，
+10.5%QoQ ，+44%YoY ， 營 業 利 益144.3 億元， +27.1%QoQ ，
+1,293%YoY ，稅後淨利 339.5 億元 ( 原估 268.4 億元 ) ，
+26.9%QoQ，+943%YoY，稅後EPS 4.28元(原估3.38元)。

6. 預估 2026 、2027 年稅後 EPS 13.82 元、 21.64 元 ， 分 別
+2,325%YoY、+56.6%YoY  
展望未來，研究部認為南亞電子材料事業持續增溫，公司整
體營運將進一步轉佳。預估 2026年營收3,416.8億元(原估
3,287.9億元)，+31.5%YoY，毛利率20.4%，+12.1pctYoY。全
年營業利益478.3億元，+1,192%YoY，本業營運明顯回升，加
上權益法認列轉投資子公司獲利大增 ， 預估2026全年稅後EPS 
13.82元(原估12.02元)，+2,325%YoY。

預估2027年營收4,533億元(原估3,788億元)，+32.7%YoY，毛
利率30.9%，+10.5pctYoY。全年營業利益1,099.5億元(原估
922億元)，+129.8%YoY， 本業營運持續提升 ， 權益法認列轉投
資子公司獲利進一步擴大 ， 預估2027全年稅後淨利1,717億元
(原估1,344億元)，+56.6%YoY，EPS 21.64元(原估16.94元)。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 30 日

1 群益投顧 
金像電 (2368 TT) 於 09/29/2026 董事會核准總金額 79 億 元 的 資 本 支 出 預 算 案 ， 預 計 自
4Q26 起 陸 續 進 行 投 資，並 決 議 洽 定 特 定 應 募 人 為Amazon.com, Inc(AMZN US) ，實 際
私募價格為 856 元 ， 預 計 此 次 私 募 規 模 為 15.89 億元、占金像電 2Q26 股本 0.36% 。
研 究 部 認 為，金 像 電 作 為 既 有 ASIC 伺 服 器 板 的 生 產 廠 商，隨 著 CSP 業者未來資本支
出 逐 步 放 大 、 對 算 力 的 要 求 也 直 接 提 升 PCB 板 規 格 與 產 品 種 類 ， 金 像 電 也 勢 必 增 加
資 本 支 出 以 維 持 產 業 競 爭 力 。 綜 合 以 上 ， 考 量 新 產 品 、 新 業 務 、 新 產 能 將 持 續 挹 注 金
像電 2027 年的營運，故維持金像電 Buy 的 投 資 建 議 ， 目 標 價 1,480 元 (22.70 PER X 
2027F EPS) 。

研 究 員 ： 吳 建 弘   
A51102@cim.capital.com.tw

金 像 電 09/29 /2026 董 事 會 核 准 資 本 支 出 案 與 公 告 第 一 次 私 募 案 對 象：  
金像電 (2368 TT) 於 09/29/2026 董 事 會 核 准 總 金 額 79 億 元 的 資 本 支 出 預 算
案 ， 預 計 自 4Q26 起 陸 續 進 行 投 資 ， 主 要 進 行 38 億 元 的 土 地 預 算 、41 億元
的 建 廠 與 設 備 資 本 支 出 。 
並決議 2026 年 第 一 次 私 募 普 通 股 定 價 及 應 募 人 資 訊 ，已 洽 定 特 定 應 募 人 為
Amazon.com, Inc(AMZN US) ， 預 計 本 次 私 募 增 資 認 購 股 數 為 1,856,308 股，
實 際 私 募 價 格為 856 元 。 此 次 私 募 規 模 為 15.89 億 元 、 占 金 像 電 2Q26 股本
0.36% 。  
研 究 部 評 析 
參考 04/16/2026 金 像 電 決 議 辦 理 私 募 增 資 案 的 內 容 ， 此 次Amazon 入股，
預 計 將 擴 大金 像 電 的 伺 服 器 業 務 範 圍 ， 提 高 營 運 績 效 。而 金 像 電 作 為既有
ASIC 伺 服 器 板 的 生 產 廠 商 ， 隨 著 CSP 業 者 未 來 資 本 支 出 逐 步 放 大 、 對 算
力 的 要 求 直 接 提 升 PCB 板 規 格 與 產 品 種 類 ， 金 像 電 也 勢 必 增 加 資 本 支 出 以
維 持 產 業 競 爭 力 。 
展望 2027 年 ， 隨 著 新世代的 AI ASIC 伺 服 器 產 品 逐 漸 量 產 ， 新 產 品 、 新
業務 、 新 產 能 將 持 續 挹 注金 像 電 營 運 ， 推 升 後 續 展 望 。 而 09/29/2026 的資
本 支 出 案 ， 研 究 部 基 於 設 備 交 期 規 劃 ， 預 計 將 投 入2028 年後 的 營運。  
綜 合 以 上 ， 考 量新 產 品 、 新 業 務 、 新 產 能 將 持 續 挹 注 金 像 電2027 年的營
運， 故 維 持 金 像 電 Buy 的 投 資 建 議 ， 目 標 價 1,480 元 (22.70 PER X 2027F 
EPS) 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

AVC (3017.TW): Liquid cooling rising adoption along with new AI servers 
across GPU and ASIC to drive sequential monthly revenues growth; Buy
25 September 2026 | 7:51PM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect AVC’s monthly revenues to continue 
sequential growth through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at 
+18% / +27% QoQ, driven by (1) new AI server racks ramp up, driving overall liquid 
cooling penetration rate. As we highlighted in June (report link), we expect AI server 
rack (NVL72-equivalent) shipment (incl. Nvidia and AMD) to reach 55k/ 105k/ 163k 
in 2026-28E, increasing by 16% / 20% in 2027 / 28E, reflecting our positive view on 
AI server racks, (2) its major ASIC AI server customer’s new model ramp up, driving its 
air-cooling and liquid-cooling components, along with a rising liquid cooling 
penetration rate, (3) its customer base expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further 
drive its liquid cooling components in 4Q26, and (4) increasing adoption rate of 
ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit the lightweight design and major 
customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s net income to grow at a +39% 
CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a growing liquid cooling 
penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total solutions (air cooling, 
liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain Buy.

Earnings revisions: We factor in AVC’s Aug revenue and raise our 2026-28E net

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$4,513 on a target P/E multiple of 30.3x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Asus (2357.TW): AI server racks in expansion along with PC product mix 
upgrade; Aug beat; Neutral
27 September 2026 | 10:33AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep, leading 
3Q26 revenues to grow at 8% QoQ, while we expect sequential revenues decline in 
4Q26 at 7%, considering the PC consumption pull in under the high memory costs 
along with AI servers model transition. We remain positive on the company’s 
expansion toward AI servers and expect the upcoming new models carrying higher 
ASP to further drive its growth in late 2026 / early 2027. We continue to expect 
double digit revenues YoY in 3Q26 / 4Q26E at 39% / 27% YoY driven by PC product 
mix upgrade and AI server racks ramp up. The company retains strong commitments 
on capacity expansion and diversification, with issuance of US$0.5bn of convertible 
bonds and US$1bn of exchangeable bonds, supporting the expansion. Maintain 
Neutral on fair valuation.

Earnings revision: We factor in ASUS’s Aug revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
incomes by 4% / 7% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues. 2026-28E revenues are raised 
by 4% / 4% / 4%, mainly on robust AI server growth momentum, and PC product mix 
upgrade along with rising selling price under the high memory costs.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Asus 
We are Neutral-rated on ASUS with a 12-month target price of NT$1,115. Our target 
price is based on 14.8x 2027E P/E, which is derived from brand makers’ average ratio of 
2027E P/E to 2028E NI YoY and OPM. We see EPS growth and OPM as major factors for 
the stock’s share performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market 
growth; 2) faster / slower-than-expected AI and gaming PC growth; and 3) faster / 
slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 24 Sep 2026 close.

Idea
Current debate: Can MediaTek meet Street 2028 forecasts given Intel EMIB-T
supply issue? Intel's EMIB-T substrate supply has been a potential bottleneck for
future TPU v9 ("Humufish") shipments in 2028, making some investors reluctant to
own MediaTek. The issue stems from a supply-demand mismatch. We forecast 3mn
TPU v9 units, as demand from Google (covered by Brian Nowak) remains strong.
However, as discussed in our AI Supply Chain Tracker, we expect only 2mn EMIB-T

substrates, i.e. 40–45k wafer-equivalent EMIB-T capacity in 2028. This aligns with
Digitimes' report that Google is considering CoWoS as a backup for its 2027 TPU on
lagging EMIB-T substrate yields.  For TSMC, supporting CoWoS could also help
regain market share from EMIB-T. Our checks indicate that both MediaTek and
TSMC are engaging to meet customer (Google) demand.
TSMC's willingness to support production increases confidence in our aggressive

2028 EPS forecast: Assuming TSMC CoWoS-L can support 0.5mn TPU v9 units and
EMIB-T can support 2.5mn units, our 3mn-unit target for 2028 appears highly
achievable, with a higher ASP from the CoWoS version. CoWoS-L carries a
structurally higher cost, and the RDL (redistribution layer) interposer connection
requires tape-out of three additional photomask layers. However, our checks also
suggest that Google is willing to pay these incremental costs to secure dual

sourcing, which should help MediaTek sustain its gross margin.
New debate: Who could be the second CSP customer? Investors have asked
whether MediaTek could win Tesla's AI6 edge AI chip program. We believe AI6 is
better suited to GUC, given its 10%–25% margin profile. Considering the higher tech
content of MediaTek's design services, incl. IP + design capabilities, we believe
MediaTek targets a 30%–35% margin profile. Instead, we see a growing probability

that MediaTek wins a cloud AI ASIC project from an AI-focused CSP and/or AI lab
customer through its  Nvidia / NVLink Fusion partnership. In previous notes, we
highlighted interest from both SpaceX and an AI lab customer. The customer's
decision could come in 4Q26 and serve as a significant catalyst for MediaTek. With
TPU upside and a potential new project, we expect MediaTek AI ASIC-related
revenue of US$13.5bn and US$54bn in 2027 and 2028, resp., representing c.38% and

72% of total revenue. Reiterate Top Pick.

### Page 2

Idea
2
TSMC CoWoS capacity support eases concerns regarding
MediaTek 2028  TPU production
Smartphone market still lukewarm into 2027, but can serve as a long-term option
value: At our recent China Best Conference, meetings involving China smartphone OEM
and NAND module makers appeared to indicate China's smartphone demand may not
recover in 2027, as mobile DRAM prices may not be falling anytime soon. We expect
MediaTek’s 3Q26 revenue to be ahead of guidance because of customer pull-in to avoid

the SoC price hike rather than due to demand improvement.
That said, once AI smartphones become a killer application, MediaTek should rank among
the best-positioned semiconductor companies to provide the edge computing capabilities
required in handsets. We also believe 2028 should be a strong year for MediaTek's
smartphone SoC business.
1. Google TPU supply chain update
TSMC CoWoS may  support both 3nm (Zebrafish) and 2nm (Humufish) MediaTek TPU
in 2028: In our earlier report, MediaTek: 2Q26 results preview: Smartphone weak, but

Cloud AI taking off; OW (26 Jul 2026), we noted that  MediaTek may secure a portion of
CoWoS capacity for 2nm TPU to ensure minimum volume production. But, we believe it
will use EMIB-T from Intel (covered by Joseph Moore)  as the primary solution to reduce
costs and scale volume in 2028, if execution proceeds well. Our recent industry checks
suggest TSMC CoWoS would continue to support both 3nm (Zebrafish) and 2nm
(Humufish) MediaTek TPU in 2028. We now assume around 500k units of 2nm TPU going

through CoWoS production as a second source. The 3nm TPU in 2028 of around 2mn
units is higher than our initial expectation of 1mn units. We therefore raise our MediaTek
2028 TPU revenue assumption to US$51.5bn.
2nm TPU TAM is supported by different SKU: At our AI Summit, MediaTek management
stated that its design service is capable of both training and inference design. Recently,
investors have been asking whether MediaTek is involved in the design of 2nm TriggerFish

TPU, intended for AI inference. Our supply chain checks suggest this could be true. The
TriggerFish chip has more features (I/O die upgrade, one more simulation die, and change
of HBM controller to HBM4e), so we think MediaTek’s 2nm TPU blended ASP could go
higher. We estimate ASP at US$12-20k (we now model US$13k). But again, the EMIB-T
substrate yield should be the key risk to volume.
CoWoS constraint eased, but ABF substrate supply still the gating factor for TPU bull

case: In our recent AI supply chain tracker, we noted that MediaTek’s 180k CoWoS-S
booking implies ~3.6mn units of TPU 8t (ZebraFish) in 2027, above our original 3mn
shipment assumption. However, we continue to bake in some conservatism as we had
assumed ABF substrate shortages. So,  we still forecast 3mn units in 2027.

2. Who could be the second and third customer for MediaTek
in AI ASICs?
NVLink Fusion partnership should be the key catalyst for MediaTek to win new
projects:
Through the NVLink Fusion platform, MediaTek's XPU customers can leverage NVIDIA's
scale-up and scale-out networking technologies to build AI server systems.
In our report, MediaTek: NVIDIA and Alphabet Invest in MediaTek’s CB for Extensive
Partnership in ASIC; Stay OW, Top Pick (31 Aug 2026), we shared that MediaTek had

maintained its 2027 ASIC TAM and market share guidance despite the broader partnership,
noting that commercialization would take time. However, we believe the partnership
materially increases MediaTek's chances of winning a second CSP customer.
Overall, we see upside to our 2028 ASIC revenue assumptions, driven by: 1) a higher
probability of winning a second or even third CSP customer; 2) greater revenue from
material preparation; and 3) a strengthened partnership with Alphabet on TPU programs.

In today's research, we incorporate potential revenue contributions from new customers.
Exhibit 2: NVLink Fusion to support connection with custom ASIC
Source: Nvidia
MediaTek's second CSP customer could begin contributing revenue from subsystem
builds in 2028: We believe the subsystem would include procurement of key components
such as HBM and ABF substrates, as well as wafer foundry capacity prepayments.

### Page 4

4
Our checks suggest MediaTek may target subsystem builds for a second CSP customer
rather than supplying the XPU itself. We now expect an initial revenue contribution in
2028, based on approximately 100k subsystem units at an ASP of US$25k.
We therefore assume a US$2.5bn revenue contribution in 2028, based on 100k units and
an ASP of US$25k, including HBM chip consignment, known-good dies (KGDs) for
compute chips, and chiplets.
On margin trends, management highlighted continued value creation for customers across

both silicon and packaging design. As a result, we expect ASICs to remain accretive to
operating margins. At the gross margin level, management indicated that margins vary by
project, potentially reflecting support for full-rack systems. We model 2nm TPU gross
margins at c.35% (vs. c.40% for 3nm TPUs) given the larger revenue scale. We continue to
expect operating margins of ~20–25%, above the smartphone business unit's 15–20%.

3. Why TSMC is motivated  to support MediaTek and Google?
TSMC's CoWoS-L solution carries a structurally higher cost due to its larger reticle size, as
we discussed in The Foundry Floorplan: TSMC preview update; CoWoS vs. EMIB
competition (14 Apr 2026). We believe TSMC also wants to address growing competition
from Intel's EMIB-T, which we estimate will reach 40-45k wafer equivalent capacity 2028,

implying a 10-15% share of the 2.5D packaging market.
For MediaTek to adopt TSMC's CoWoS-L as a second source alongside EMIB-T, the RDL
(redistribution layer) connection to the interposer requires tape-out of three additional
photomask layers. However, our checks suggest that Google is willing to absorb the
incremental costs associated with dual sourcing, which should allow MediaTek to sustain
its gross margin.

Morgan Stanley Research 9
Relative valuation
We view the P/E as relatively attractive – even after the share price rally – given strong AI
demand. Although MediaTek faces smartphone headwinds in 2026, we believe it is time to
look beyond 2026. We think this margin pressure is fully priced in, while TPU upside is
not. We believe the stock should trade more than +1SD above its historical average,
supported by strong TPU demand. Our  price target of NT$6,188 implies 42x  2027 EPS, on

our estimates.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 2

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
2
•MediaTek’s advanced-node and supply chain capabilities are underestimated — We believe MediaTek’s Google
TPU order can scale materially into 2028 and beyond despite market concerns regarding possible project delays. The
TPU V8 chip is already in production, and we believe it is on track for US$2bn revenue in 2026. MediaTek has also
consistently been a top 5 customer at TSMC for the most advanced node with big volumes for several years. They

have multiple N2 projects and early work extending toward A14. This matters because leading-edge ASIC execution
is not only about SerDes, on which MediaTek is also catching up with global peers; the RTL design would require
advanced node design and technology co-optimization, physical implementation, foundry capacity management,
package and substrate coordination, testing, and high-volume ramp. Under Google’s COT/semi-custom model, this
capability should become even more relevant as MediaTek provides design, integration, and manufacturing

execution.
•EMIB-T risk for TPU v9, yet CoWoS/CoPoS for v10 likely to succeed — While investors are also skeptical about
MediaTek’s SerDes technology, we note that its 224G SerDes is in production and that 336G and 448G SerDes is
actively under development. MediaTek is also developing die-to-die, which has already been silicon-validated on
TSMC N3P and N2P. For advanced packaging, we would nevertheless not dismiss EMIB-T yield risk for TPU v9 and

large-scale chiplet integration. Yet we believe there is strong chance that MediaTek will be working on
CoWoS/CoPoS for TPU v10, which is aiming for 2029 production. And its higher value for v9 and long lift v8 should
be more than enough to compensate for the potential shipment downside into 2028.
•Growing with expanding TPU opportunity + new customer engagement — There have been reports that Marvell,
AMD, and Samsung Foundry are potential alternatives for high-end HBM4E inference TPU and future TPU V10

designs. In our view, Google’s expanding TPU TAM should support multiple suppliers and TPU V10 is more likely a
2029–2030 generation. We believe MediaTek and TSMC will continue to be the main source. Given MediaTek’s tight
relationship with Nvidia and the NVLink Fusion ecosystem, we believe it has other hyperscale opportunities such as
Microsoft and xAI.
•Maintaining Buy; more than doubling AI revenue into 2028 from 2027 — In our view, Mediatek is emerging as a

credible hyperscale ASIC partner with materially stronger advanced-node, chiplet, SerDes, and supply-chain
capabilities than the bear case implies. EMIB-T and first-generation large-chiplet execution remain the key risks, but
MediaTek’s rapid learning curve, manufacturing scale, cost discipline, and Google’s capacity-sharing model provide
meaningful mechanisms to mitigate them. We estimate MediaTek’s TPU shipment to be at 4m units for 2027 and
2028, but 2028 revenue to double compared to 2027 due to higher ASP on upgraded SerDes (336G) and N2

advanced node top die design effort.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 4

Mediatek (2454.TW)
29 September 2026 Citi Research
4
Mediatek
Valuation
Our NT$6,800 target price (rounded) for MediaTek is based on 35x P/E to 2027/2028 EPS, which is 1.5-std above its five-year
average forward P/E given the promising outlook from its AI ASIC business starting from 2H26. We believe our target PE is also
justified by Mediatek's global and regional peers' trading at an average of 36x PER. AI ASIC development with CSPs should
provide more upside from 2027 onwards. We also see Mediatek well prepared for 6G and 6G+NTN Satellite communication.

Despite lukewarm smartphone overall deamand, we expect Mediatek to benefit from 5G migration in emerging markets and its
Dimensity series should also gain shares in flagship models. Non-smartphone business is also seeing stabilizing TV and IoT
demand with Mediatek outgrowing the overall market on its better technology in WiFi 7 and connectivity.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected growth from

AI ASIC business; 2) more severe competition in the smartphone segment; 3) slower overall demand related to macroeconomic
risks, especially for smartphone demand; and 4) further price hikes from foundries.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

and GM likely improving sequentially in Q3, we believe Q3 EPS is tracking above
expectations.

ChipMOS Technologies 29 September 2026 ab 2

Company Description
ChipMOS is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 45-50% of its sales from display driver
IC (~40% from TV and 40% from smartphone and IT and 20% from automotive), 35-40%
from memory (~40% DRAM, ~40% from specialty flash and ~20% from mainstream NAND)

and 10-15% from logic (TV SoC) and mixed signal IC (MEMS, PMIC).
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We have a Buy rating and a NT$166 price target, based on 23x average 2027-28E PE (vs. its
normalized 5-27x PE range). Our target multiple is in line with the average of memory and
driver IC OSAT peers.Downside risks include a weaker-than-expected tech end-market
demand recovery, growing China competition, failure to diversify into the SiPh/optical

component bumping business and a slower ramp of its logic testing business.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 3

NYPCB (8046.TW)
30 September 2026 Citi Research
3
BT substrate: improving industrywide supply with sustainable memory demand — In
the near term, while we do observe fading BT demand from smartphone, we also see
robust memory demand likely help offset the weakness in smartphone. T o note that,
NYPCB’s memory sales mix has increased to >60% currently from 30% in the past.
Although the market was disappointed about NYPCB’s muted BT price hike in 4Q26,

we think its BT demand, especially from memory products, is likely to be more resilient
than expected, with potential upsides in 4Q26E or 1Q27E. In the medium term, we also
observe Unimicron and SEMCO (report here) are converting its BT capacity into ABF
one, which would gradually benefit the industrywide BT supply side from 4Q26
onward. More meaningful outflowing BT orders would likely occur after 2Q27 with the
last BT orders placed by customers to Unimicron and SEMCO.

Demand continues to build up; upward earnings revisions; Buy — We see
favorable ABF/BT trend for NYPCB in the upcoming quarters. For its PCB segment
(with demand mostly from non-AI applications now), it seems to be stabilizing in
the past few quarters and likely sees some upsides from new applications. Thus,
we raise our 2026/27 /28E earnings by 2%/8%/6% to factor better demand and
price hikes on supply tightness. We raise our TP to NT$1,700 (30x 2027E EPS) from

NT$1,600 (30x 2027E EPS) and reiterate our Buy rating. We also open 90-day
upside CW for NYPCB, as we expect AI chip production ramp along with price hikes
would drive NYPCB upcoming monthly sales momentum in 4Q26E.

NYPCB (8046.TW)

NYPCB
(8046.TW; NT$ 1340.0; 1; 30 Sep 26; 13:30)
Company description
Established in 1997, Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board (NYPCB) is a technology
subsidiary of the Formosa Plastics Group. Nan Ya PCB's main products
include conventional PCB, HDI, Flip Chip substrate (FCBGA and FCCSP), Wire
Bond Substrate, etc. The company has manufacturing sites in Taiwan and
Kunshan, China.
Investment strategy
We rate NYPCB shares as Buy. We believe ABF/BT should see a solid recovery

in 2026 on robust AI demand: 1) NYPCB is the purest ABF play with a
significant earnings turnaround, driven by growing chip sizes and layer counts
for AI ASIC/networking; and 2) the BT business should benefit from price
hikes on rising memory demand and T glass shortage. Considering T glass
support from NYPCB’s parent company, we believe NYPCB would  have a
better chance to win additional AI ASIC/networking business going forward.
Valuation
Our DCF-based TP for NYPCB is NT$1,700. With a risk-free rate of 5.00% (10-

year government bond rate), a market risk premium of 7% and an equity beta
of 1.22 (YTD weekly share price), we calculate NYPCB's WACC as 13.4%. Our
TP is equivalent to 30x/13x 2027E EPS/BVPS, which reflects its high P/E
multiple during upcycles. NYPCB traded at 15-20x P/E during its 2003-2008
upcycle driven by PC/NB demand strength. In 2010-11 when the market was
recovering from the global financial crisis, NYPCB traded at 25-35x P/E. In the
last 2019-22 upcycle when ABF was in shortage, NYPCB traded at 30-40x

P/E.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the NYPCB shares from reaching our
target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected ABF demand from key
customers; 2) BT business seasonality weakness and fewer-than-expected
price hikes; and 3) severe T glass shortages that cause a bottleneck to its
production.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned

Source PDF: all report pdf/260930_daiwa_delta.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our views on Delta after c.4 months of share price 
weakness, driven by investor concerns on HVDC delays, and a slowdown 
in power consumption trend amid deceleration in Nvidia’s GPU shipment 
growth YoY. We remain positive on Delta over 2026-28E, as rising AI ASIC 
server trend should drive a meaningful increase in DC-DC power module 
ASPs (vs. Nvidia’s discrete form factor). We expect higher HVDC adoption 
from 2028E, driven by IT rack and power supply architecture changes.

What’s the impact: Component shortages are the key near-term 
bottleneck. We attribute the recent share-price weakness to slower-than-
expected revenue momentum in both 2Q26, due to vendor-sanction issue 
at Delta Thailand, and 3Q26E, due to a worsening shortage of power 
semiconductors. We believe the 2Q26 revenue shortfall was a one-off 
event, though 3Q26E growth is likely to remain constrained by the power-
semi shortage. While supply capacity should improve, the supply-demand 
gap will likely persist for some time. That said, end-demand should remain 
sound. Given the continued improvement in revenue momentum, we still 
expect operating margin to reach a record-high of 18.3% in 3Q26.

Long-term growth drivers. We see Delta as a key beneficiary of the AI 
ASIC server trend. We were previously concerned that rising AI ASIC 
adoption could hurt Delta, as ASICs have lower thermal design power 
(TDP) than Nvidia GPUs, but now believe that Delta will benefit from rising 
content value of DC-DC power modules, as hyperscalers are more willing 
to spend on DC-DC modules (vs. discrete) despite higher initial cost given 
their superior energy efficiency. We forecast DC-DC power module revenue 
to reach c.TWD60bn in 2026E (vs. TWD9bn in 2025 and our prior estimate 
of c.TWD20bn for 2026E). This figure could increase further with rising 
ASIC usage in 2027. We also believe HVDC adoption rate could reach at 
least 50% in 2028E (vs. our prior expectation of 30%), as power supply 
architecture is becoming increasingly bulky (to accommodate aluminium 
capacitors inside power supply amid the super capacitor shortage). Limited 
space in IT racks would force customers to adopt more power racks and 
likely equip them with high voltage versions to improve efficiency.

What we recommend: We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 2-15% on higher 
HVDC contribution. We lift our 12M TP to TWD3,088 (from TWD2,988) 
based on a lower target PER of 49x (from 55x) to reflect market sentiment, 
(at the high-end of its past-3-year range of 11-50x) applied to our 1-year-
forward EPS. Key downside risk: lower-than-expected demand.

How we differ: Our 2028E earnings is 21% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish assumption for HVDC adoption.

30 September 2026

Key beneficiary of the AI ASIC server trend

➢ Better-than-expected DC-DC AI server contribution from 2026E 
➢ HVDC adoption rate should be higher-than-expected in 2028E 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating and raising 12M TP to TWD3,088

Company profile 
Founded in 1971, Delta Electronics has been the global leader in switching power supply solutions since 
2002 and DC brushless fans since 2006. Its business covers 3 key areas: power electronics (eg, power 
supplies, thermal management products and passive components), automation (eg, controllers, robot 
arms and building management systems), and infrastructure (eg, datacentre infrastructure, networking 
and EV charging).

### Page 5

5

Delta Electronics (2308 TT): 30 September 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 30 Sep 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
Delta's board has 7 non-independent and 5 independent directors with a term of three years.
The total number of directors and ratio of independent directors appear higher than most other
listed companies. Besides, according to Delta's CSR report, enhancing the board's functions is
one of the key goals for the company to achieve better corporate governance; also, the board
members' diversity and professionalism will be taken into consideration to ensure a good board
quality. Moreover, the company evaluates all the directors in the board using internal regulations
and an external independent institution each year and at least once every three years,
respectively. We believe Delta has implemented relatively more enhanced measure than most
listed firms to improve its board quality.
Capital management 2
During 2016-19, Delta's payout ratio was stable in the range of c.70%, but fell to 56% in 2020
and 53% in 2021. For 2020, it was mainly due to a sizeable non-cash re-valuation gain after the
acquisition of Delta Thailand (DET), while the company maintained more cash on hand to deal
with the uncertainties caused by COVID-19. However, the DPS growth from 2016 to 2021 was
limited, as Delta struggled with business diversification into new end-markets (eg, smartphone,
NEV). We believe that Delta's effort started to bear fruit in recent years after its NEV business
has gradually picked up. Overall, we see a stable dividend policy from Delta and expect its DPS
to continue to grow YoY in the future given that they declared a TWD11.6 DPS for 2025 which
equals a 50% payout ratio.
Related party &
transaction 1 Revenue from related parties stood at 12% of total 2025 revenue and purchases from related
parties were at 11%. We see limited risk from related party transactions.
S Supply chain
management 1
Delta focuses on: 1) localised management, 2) supplier certification and risk management, and
3) supplier sustainability engagement as key initiatives under the scope of supply chain
management. In 2019, 144 new suppliers joined Delta's supply chain system. All of them passed
environmental and social evaluations, signed the declaration of "non-use of conflict materials",
and complied with Responsible Business Alliance's (BRA) Code of Conduct. Also, these vendors
had to implement and abide by Delta's supply chain CSR policy and code of conduct. To lower
ESG risks, Delta carries out RBA audits and monitors high-risk critical suppliers each year. The
suppliers that fail these audits have to provide detailed improvement plans in response to
identified issues. Besides, Delta has surveyed a total of 263 suppliers with potential risk and 63
suppliers with high risk of the total 1,331 suppliers, based on its CSR policy and code of conduct.
If these suppliers are flagged as high-risk and no concrete improvements are made, Delta may
consider terminating cooperation and transactions with them to improve the company's
engagement on supplier sustainability. Overall, we think Delta has a solid plan to improve its
supply chain management with specific KPIs, including: 1) achieving supplier improvement rate
of 80% in 2025, 2) reducing GHG emissions rate of critical suppliers by 10% in 2025, and 3)
improving water saving rate of critical suppliers by 12% in 2025.
S Data security 2
Delta's Information Security team created Delta's Information Security Policy in November 2013
and started providing educational training to global employees in 2014. Delta gradually
introduced the Information Security Management System (ISMS) in 2016 and obtained the ISO
27001 international certification in 2018. According to ISO27001, Delta has to conduct at least
one internal audit on information security and appoint an independent institution to conduct audit
externally. According to Delta, no major deficiencies have been found in recent years.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

TSMC will host its 3Q26 analyst meeting on 15 October. We expect a 
strong finish to 2026 with a sustained growth outlook into 2027 for 
TSMC as demand broadens across AI GPUs/AI ASICs, networking 
and server CPUs. In particular, the stronger CPU demand driven by 
agentic AI represents a key change over the past year. We forecast 
2026E/2027E revenue growth of 42.0%/36.9% YoY (USD terms), 
with GM at 67.2%/67.5%. We also raise our 2027E/2028E CapEx 
forecasts to US$85bn/US$98bn (vs. US$78bn/US$82bn 
previously) as we expect TSMC to further ramp up its capacity 
investment to support longer-term customer demand. 
Strong sequential growth into 4Q26 with modest N2 ramp dilution 
We expect TSMC’s 3Q26E revenue to grow 15.3% QoQ, followed 
by a further 11.0% QoQ increase in 4Q26E (both in USD terms), 
supported by continued strength in AI/HPC demand. On 
profitability, we model GM edging down to 67.5%/67.3% in 
3Q26E/4Q26E, vs. 67.7% in 2Q26, reflecting dilution from the 
ongoing N2 ramp.  
Momentum sustaining into 2027, supported by broader AI demand 
We expect TSMC’s revenue to grow 36.9% YoY (vs. 32.0% previously) 
in USD terms in 2027E, supported by sustained demand for AI 
accelerators and networking, alongside an increasingly strong 
contribution from server CPUs. At our Communacopia + Technology 
conference, management highlighted that lower token costs should 
stimulate greater AI consumption, while discussions with customers 
and US CSPs has reinforced confidence in demand and 
infrastructure readiness. We believe these trends support continued 
strength in leading-edge demand, particularly for both N2 and N3, 
where management expects supply to remain insufficient despite 
further expansion.

### Page 3

On the supply side, we expect capacity additions, N5-to-N3 conversion, and 
productivity gains to support higher wafer output, while continued CoWoS expansion 
helps enable associated wafer shipments. We model GM improving modestly to 67.5% in 
2027E (vs. 67.2% in 2026E), supported by higher pricing, favorable product mix, 
sustained high utilization and continued productivity gains. 
Higher CapEx to support longer-term expansion 
We maintain our 2026E CapEx forecast at US$64bn, while raising our 2027E/2028E 
forecasts to US$85bn/US$98bn (vs. US$78bn/US$82bn previously), to incorporate 1) 
cost inflation from equipment vendors, and 2) initial spending associated with the 
potential new Texas expansion; however, we expect the new site to only enter mass 
production beyond 2032. For capacity, we continue to model our end-2027E/2028E 
N3/N2 capacity at 200kwpm/140kwpm and 220kwpm/200kwpm, respectively. 
Other key areas in focus for the upcoming analyst meeting 
Long-term AI growth outlook: We believe the broadening of AI demand across 
accelerators, CPUs and networking further supports TSMC’s long-term growth 
opportunity. We would look for updated comments from management on the key 
assumptions underpinning its AI outlook through 2030, where demand drivers have 
strengthened most over the past year, and the factors that could materially change this 
outlook. 
Longer-term CapEx outlook and US expansion: With customer demand continuing to 
drive investment in leading-edge capacity and advanced packaging, we would look for 
management to provide further direction on its longer-term CapEx trajectory and the 
demand visibility supporting these investment decisions. We would also welcome 
updated comments on whether customer demand could lead to further expansion in the 
US beyond current Arizona site, and the potential timing and scale of such investments. 
Foundry competition and technology leadership: We would look for management’s 
perspective on the evolving competitive landscape, including Intel, Samsung and 
Terafab, and the implications for TSMC’s longer-term positioning. In particular, we would 
welcome further discussion of its technology leadership, and whether management 
expects this gap to widen or narrow across upcoming process generations.

Earnings estimate revisions, valuation and risks

Forecast changes 
We have revised up 2026/27/28E earnings by 1%/7%/8% to reflect 1) higher GM 
assumptions as we see a milder GM dilution from N2 ramp, and 2) higher UTRs especially 
for N2/N3 given stronger demand from AI/HPC.

Reiterate Buy; TP raise to NT$3,300 from NT$3,100 
We raise our 12m TP to NT$3,300 from NT$3,100 (2330.TW), following our upward 
earnings revisions. Our TP is based on a target P/E of 22x (unchanged, benchmarked to 
1.0stdv above 5-year avg. forward P/E) applied to our higher 2027E EPS. Accordingly, we 
raise our 12 month TP for the ADR to US$660 from US$620, based on an unchanged FX

### Page 7

Investment Thesis - TSMC (2330.TW/TSM) 
TSMC is a leading global foundry company specializing in cutting-edge nodes. Its leading 
technology stance enables it to enjoy more than 60% revenue share in the global 
foundry market. We like TSMC as we believe its solid technology leadership and 
execution better position it vs. peers to capture the industry’s long-term structural 
growth opportunities, particularly in areas such as AI/5G/HPC/EV. In addition, valuation 
looks attractive in our view, with the shares trading at the mid-range of their 10-year 
trading history (P/E: 10-29x). Furthermore, we view TSMC as the key AI enabler among 
our Taiwan Semis coverage, thanks to its leadership stance in leading edge nodes and 
advanced packaging technology – CoWoS (chip on wafer on substrate). We believe TSMC 
will achieve its 25% revenue CAGR target for the next several years, driven primarily by 
increasing silicon content growth and AI/HPC demand, with LT GM to remain at 56%+. 
We are Buy rated.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - TSMC (2330.TW/TSM) 
Valuation methodology: We have a 12m TP of NT$3,300, which is derived by applying a 
target P/E multiple of 22x to our 2027E EPS. Our 22x target P/E is benchmarked against 
1.0stdv above its 5-year trading average. For the ADR (TSM), we have a 12m TP of 
US$660, based on a USD/TWD rate of 30.0 and an ADR premium of 20%. 
Key downside risks to our views: (1) further deterioration in end-demand recovery 
impacting capacity utilization; (2) slower customer node migrations; (3) slowdown in AI 
investment resulting in lower long-term semiconductor content growth; (4) poor 
yields/execution resulting in worse-than-expected profitability; (5) stronger 
competition resulting in ASP/profitability erosion; and (6) unfavorable FX trend or 
higher-than-expected cost increase weighing on the margin outlook.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

股價相對大盤走勢

台勝科 (3532 TT)  Trading Buy

營運逐步回升，建議 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ：台勝科為日商 SUMCO(3436 JP) 、台塑 (1301 TT) 、亞
太投資合資成立的專業 8 吋及 12 吋矽晶圓製造商，資本額 38.8 
億元。先前 SUMCO 處份部份台勝科，目前持有股權約 38% ，
台塑集團及其轉投資子公司亞太投資合計持有 38%。AI 需求推
動資料中心建置，相關 12 吋先進製程用晶圓需求強勁，公司持
續擴大銷售 12 吋先進製程矽晶圓、導入 SUMCO 12 吋先進製
程矽晶圓技術並與客戶合作開發高階製程應用，朝高階邏輯矽
晶圓布局。也布局 CoWoS 先進封裝、 3D 封裝與 HBM 矽晶圓。
預估台勝科 2026 年稅後 EPS 2.19 元。整體矽晶圓受惠高階製
程和 DRAM 矽晶圓需求佳，再加上漲價效應，有望帶動營運逐
步向上，建議 Trading Buy ，目標價 489 元 (2027 年 PER65X) 。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 0.12 元：受惠於 AI 算力基礎建設推動記憶體與
先進邏輯製程需求，客戶產能利用率維持高檔， 2Q26 營收為
36.10 億元， QoQ+9.11% ， 12 吋矽晶圓佔營收比重 >70% 、其餘
為 8 吋矽晶圓。 8 吋矽晶圓主要客戶為晶圓代工，因 AI 伺服器
帶動電源管理 IC 與驅動 IC 補貨需求，產能利用率已達 90% 以
上。毛利率方面，雖因新廠折舊與營運成本上升壓力，但公司
正積極與客戶溝通價格調漲機制，此外，現貨價格回升， ASP 上
揚 ， 產能利用率改善 ，2Q26 毛利率增加 6.06 個百分點至 6.90%，
2Q26 後 EPS 為 0.12 元，轉虧為盈。  
麥寮新廠客戶認證將在 2026 年內完成： 台勝科 12 吋麥寮新廠
總投資額為新台幣 282.6 億元，已於 2025 年底完工。預估 2026
全年資本支出為 33 億元，包含新廠尾款約為 10.27 億元，新廠
設備調整，節能改善工程以及例行性設備汰舊換新。新廠營運
初期處於產能提升與客戶認證階段，較高的新廠折舊攤提費用
(2025 年平均 5.2 億 /季， 1H26 平均 8.5 億 /季 )，疊加人工與原物
料成本的攀升，對公司短期的毛利率與營業利益造成壓抑。不
過麥寮 12 吋新廠產能目前已被客戶預約完售，預估隨著客戶認
證逐步於 2026 年內完成，將擴大對高階產品與記憶體客戶的供
應，推升中長期營收成長以抵消折舊成本的不利因素。

2 群益投顧

台勝科 ESG ：  
公司 ESG 治理架構  
專責永續小組：台塑勝高已成立「企業社會責任推動小組」，由總經理田
中恵一擔任總召集人，副總經理擔任管理代表，專責規劃與推動公司 ESG
相關項目。

永續發展委員會：自 2022 年起設永續發展委員會，制定 ESG 策略並追蹤
目標進度，與全球供應商協作推動碳排減量及永續轉型。

董事會監督： ESG 重大主題及進展每年彙整後提報董事會，由董事會確認
與利害關係人需求一致， 2024 年 6 月最近一次審查。

主要 ESG 成績與目標  
環境（ Environment ）  
減碳目標：承諾 2025 年減碳 12.5% 、 2030 年減碳 25% ，邁向 2050 年碳中
和。

氣候管理：落實溫室氣體盤查及公告，持續推動低碳製程、提高循環利用
與改善能資源效率。

污染防制：積極優化廢水處理、加強空氣污染防制措施，以減少對地方社
區的環境影響。

社會（ Social ）  
員工照顧：推動職安維護、健康促進與育才培訓，強調性別平等與多元融
合。

社區共融：積極參與地方公益、辦理社區關懷與教育回饋計畫，提升企業
社會形象。

供應鏈共識行動： 2022 年起攜手全球 79 家供應商共宣 ESG 行動，打造綠
色供應鏈。

公司治理（ Governance ）  
治理透明：設立 ESG 委員會與內部稽核制度，跨部門協作永續目標，定期
公開永續績效報告。  
誠信經營：強調合規、資訊揭露與企業誠信，增進利害關係人信任。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

日月光投控 (3711 TT)  Buy

高階封測需求持續吃緊，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：AI 與 HPC 帶動的先進封測與測試需求成長快速，日
月光投控再度上調先進封測 (LEAP) 營收 ， 將超越先前設定35 億
美元目標， 2027 年 LEAP 營收有望再增加 1 倍；一般封測成長
率預估由先前 YoY+13% 上修至 YoY+20% 。整 體ATM 業務 2026
年營收預期 YoY+35% 。 EMS 營收略低於 YoY+20% 。預估日月
光投控 2026 年稅後 EPS 17.95 元。全球超大規模資料中心正加
速升級，為支撐具備多模態能力的複雜推論，晶片設計已大量
轉向由多顆小晶片與高頻寬記憶體堆疊的系統架構，使台積電
CoWoS 產能無不應求。日月光受惠台積電訂單外溢，加上大客
戶 AMD 的次世代 Venice CPU 架構開始放量，日月光的 FOCoS
出貨向上，將帶動營收續創新高，因此對日月光投控投資建議
為 Buy ，目標價為 862 元 (2027 年 PER35X) 。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 4.71 元：日月光投控公布日月光投控 2Q26 營收
1,910.64 億元， QoQ+10.02% 、YoY+26.74% 。其中 2Q26 ATM 營
收 1,261.48 億元， QoQ+12.19% 、 YoY+36.28% 。優於日月光預
期 2Q26 IC ATM 營收 QoQ+9 ~+11% 。合併毛利率方面，產能利
用率還是主要影響毛利率的因素。日月光投控整體合併毛利率
增加 0.94 個百分點至 21.01% 。 2Q26 稅後 EPS 4.71 元。  
日月光預估 2026 年資本支出達 105 億美元左右： 高階測試產能
非常吃緊，配合客戶的需求，日月光投控再度增加 2026 年資本
支出 20 億美元，約達 105 億美元，其中 40 億美元將用於新廠
房建設 65 億美元用於設備， YoY+91% 左右。設備支出中，大約
56% 用於封裝， 40% 用於測試，其餘用於 EMS 和材料。整體而
言，大約 70% 的設備資本支出是用於先進技術，大部分將分配
給 LEAP ，特別是晶圓測試。  
折舊方面，雖部分折舊到期，但資本支出大量增加，日月光投
控預計 2026 年折舊費用 YoY+12~15% ，折舊費用將達到約新台
幣 750 億元以上。

2 群益投顧 
  日月光 ESG ：  
永續浪潮不斷， ESG 成為企業競爭力的關鍵，日月光擴大台灣先進封測布局
的同時，更持續耕耘與扎根企業社會責任，致力於發展全方位的綠色概念。
永續治理：日月光集團是負責任的企業公民，以積極的行動確保商業行為符
合專業及道德的最高標準。公司長期的經營與成功必須仰賴企業的永續發
展及其社會責任的體現。  
環境與綠色創新：日月光承諾持續增加資源再利用及減少溫室氣體與廢水
排放、廢棄物產生及化學品使用，以改善生態效益並保護環境。  
供應鏈發展：日月光秉持「積極、互信、誠正、自省」的核心價值追求持續
成長，對於供應鏈管理日月光與時俱進，從選商、運用策略執行採購品項、
供應商整合，到集結各領域資源建構平台與集團採購制度標準化，更將永續
供應落實到採購日常管理，規範供應夥伴遵守行為準則，以「價值導向」引
導供應商重視永續發展，促進產業創新。  
員工關懷與發展：日月光集團承諾提供給員工安全、健康及優質的工作環
境，確保人才多元化及保障人權，並使公司擁有高技能及經驗豐富的人力資
本。  
社會參與：日月光集團積極參與慈善行動、教育計畫和社會工作回饋社區，
將資源做最完善的分配，並發揮正面的社會影響力。日月光集團的社會參與
策略係以「環保公益」、「產學教育」、「社區營造」及「對外倡議」為四
大發展面向，於「集團永續發展委員會」下設置了「社會參與團隊 (Social 
Involvement Taskforce) 」來促動集團內社會參與相關行動，並依循 LBG 
(London Benchmark Group) 模式審視各個發展面向之相關投入、效益及其影
響，每半年進行業務績效檢視及報告。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

休閒車新訂單與電子新產品挹注成長 
 2H26 持續受惠美國 Honda 新訂單放量出貨 ， 以及越南電動機車客戶
產能供不應求的外包訂單需求 ， 營收可望逐季墊高 。 推估下半年平均
每月營收 8~8.5 億元。 
 休閒車產品 2026 年在客戶 Polaris 調整告一段落以及北美 Honda 與
哈雷新訂單開始挹注下，預期營收貢獻將成長 50%以上，2027 年北
美 Honda 還有其他車型將量產，推估可再成長 20-30%。 
 相關機櫃與散熱產品將於 2Q26 陸續少量出貨 ，2027 年將放量出貨 。
預估電子產品營收佔比年底可增至 3-5%。因應電子與大型自動化產
線需求越南百善二廠二期擴建，預計 3Q27 末完工，推估 2027 年營
收比重可達近雙位數。 
 展望 2026 年，受惠越南市場受惠發展電動二輪政策，以及受惠北美
Honda 與哈雷新訂單挹注。預期 2026 年 EPS 17.25 元。2027 年休
閒車新訂單挹注以及電子產品隨著產能開出放量 ， 預期EPS 21.3 元。 
 維持買進 ：公司受惠北美 HONDA 新訂單與電動機車客戶轉單挹
注，今明兩年營運轉好。此外，受惠供應鏈由中國轉移至越南，公司
越南廠具在地優勢取得電子新訂單 ， 隨著越南百善二廠廠能開出 ， 將
挹注未來成長動能。維持買進建議，目標價 234 元。

北美新訂單挹注，營運逐季墊高

公司 2Q26 營收 24.1 億元 ，+16.9%QoQ，+53.1%YoY， 主要為休閒車輛訂單回溫 ，
加上越南機車業務拉貨回升。在稼動率提升與產品結構轉好下，毛利率23.8%較上
季好轉，單季 EPS 4.42 元。

2H26 持續受惠美國 Honda 新訂單放量出貨 ， 以及越南電動機車客戶產能供不應求
的外包訂單需求 ， 營收可望逐季墊高 。 推估下半年平均每月營收8~8.5 億元 ，3Q26
營收 24.34 億元，+1%QoQ，+58.8%YoY，EPS 4.72 元。4Q26 為越南機車產業
旺季，預計營收 25.07 億元，+3%QoQ，+38.9%YoY，EPS 4.53 元。

1H26 主要產品營收佔比依序為機車 44.8%、 休閒車43.9%、 健康8.6%、 汽車2.6%。

機車產品方面，越南傳統機車市場為主，主要客戶為 YAMAHA 與 Honda，2025
年受到 HONDA 下修生產計劃影響，營收貢獻較前一年下滑 14%。2026 年越南河
內與胡志明市將禁止傳統燃油二輪，轉為發展電動二輪市場，雖影響傳統燃油機車
市場，但受惠政策，新的電動二輪換車需求彌補了油車下滑的缺口。  
目前客戶 Vinfast 產能吃緊釋出訂單 ，1H26 機車產品營收貢獻成長 22%， 預期2026
年機車營收可成長 16-17%。2027 年越南 Honda 將有電動二輪產品推出，有利維
持機車產品成長。

北美 Honda 與哈雷為休閒車產品
主要成長動能

汽車與健康事業轉佳

休閒車：主要客戶為 Polaris、Harley 與北美 Honda，受惠客戶為降低成本與避開
當地關稅的策略，將產線移至越南，以及美國當地非紅供應鏈政策，相關供應鏈由
中國轉向越南。而 2025 年在川普總統推動美國製造下，公司調整產線並於美國南
卡羅來納州設廠因應。2025 年受到通膨影響北美休閒車市場需求趨緩，主要客戶
Polaris 調整庫存，目前庫存水位已將至低位，自 1Q26 客戶陸續補庫存。

新訂單方面，北美 Honda ATV 零件，目前接單規劃至 2028 年都有新專案出貨。
由於 Honda ATV 在北美規模與 Polaris 相當，為未來成長的主要客戶與動能，新
訂單於 2Q26 量產，預估營收貢獻將從 2025 年的 4.9 億元大幅增至 2026 年的 
12~13 億元。此外，公司哈雷訂單於 1Q26 開始出貨，推估 2026 年可貢獻 9-10
億元，目前仍有新項目在開發，將挹注未來營運動能。

休閒車產品 2026 年在客戶 Polaris 調整告一段落以及北美 Honda 與哈雷新訂單開
始挹注下，預期營收貢獻將成長 50%以上。2027 年除了北美 Honda SXS 與 ATV
訂單外，還有其他車型將量產，加上哈雷，與歐洲、日本的新訂單，推估休閒車營
收貢獻可再成長 20-30%。

汽車相關產品取得 Toyota、Honda 與 Vinfast 電動車訂單，加上其他車廠未來也
規劃將組裝線設於越南， 公司看好未來越南與東協車用市場成長， 透過與相關供應
鏈的台商合資取得供應商資格，未來將可從業外認列轉投資收益。 2025 年受到越
南需求不如預期影響 ， 營收貢獻僅成長6%，2026 年越南車用市場回穩 ， 加上Vinfast

健康事業目前包含醫療、健身器材、E-bike 等產品，先前健身器材新產品雖取得客

產線需求越南百善二廠二期擴建，預計 3Q27 末完工，推估 2027 年營收比重可達

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

由於北美 Honda 新車型訂單貢獻顯著，帶動 3Q26 營收淡季不淡，加上
電子新業務的訂單開始貢獻，為豐祥下半年營運挹注強勁成長動能， 研
究部據此上修豐祥今年獲利 8%，預估2026 年 EPS 17.17 元(+41%YOY)。
展望 2027 年，預期在休閒車輛、電動機車新訂單以及電子新業務貢獻
下，豐祥明年業績將繼續攀高，預估 2027 年 EPS 21.09 元。研究部給
予 12 倍本益比，將目標價上調至 250 元，維持買進評等。

重點摘要：

1. 電子業務啟動第二成長曲線，2027年進入量產階段： 
豐祥將電子業務視為企業新一輪發展的核心驅動力 ， 當前以AI
伺服器機櫃為主力產品以及其他電子相關品項。 今年下半年已
陸續有新產品少量出貨。在營收比重部分，2026年電子業務約
占合併營收的3~5%。未來隨著客戶需求釋放及新項目量產，搭
配新廠房啟用，創造更多生產空間，公司規畫2H27開始以大規
模自動化模式提升產能， 預期2027年電子業務將有更多的營收
貢獻，更能增添中長期營運動能。。

2. 3Q26淡季不淡，4Q26營收再攀高，年度獲利上修： 
豐祥第三季繼續受惠北美Honda新專案出貨順暢，季度營收 表
現優於原先季減的預期， 呈現淡季不淡 ， 延續前季的強勁表現 ，
年增率保持大幅成長 ； 之後進入越南當地電動機車零件拉貨旺
季，第四季營收有望再度衝高。整體而言，在休閒車輛與電動
機車業務雙引擎帶動下，公司下半年營收動能無虞，本業獲利
維持在高檔區域並保有顯著的年成長動能 ， 加上轉投資事業獲
利成長，研究部上修豐祥今年度獲利9%，預估豐祥2026年合併
營收94.68億元(+39%YOY)，毛利率23.4%(+1.0pct)，費用率
9.4%(+0.8pct)， 預估稅後淨利11.94億元(+41%YOY)， 預估2026
年EPS 17.17元。

3. 三大成長引擎接棒，2027年業績將再創高： 
展望2027年，研究部認為豐祥將繼續保有偏強的營運動能，主
要來自於三大核心領域 ：(1)休閒車輛新訂單持續堆疊與放量 。
特別是受惠於北美供應鏈轉移效益 ， 豐祥對客戶的供應滲透率
提升，北美Honda、Harley-Davidson等客戶陸續有新機種將於
明年量產；亦有日系客戶的新訂單將提前至明年出貨，使休閒
車業務在2027年依然是貢獻整體營收增長的最主要基石；

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

(2)越南當地電動機車與電動汽車市場規模擴大，相關訂單持
續增加，為既有業務提供成長支撐 ；(3)電子新業務的實質貢
獻。豐祥切入伺服器機櫃供應鏈，經過2026年下半年的試產準
備後，2027年將迎來訂單放量，預期電子業務佔營收比重躍升
至5%以上。此外，休閒車輛占比提高，產品組合將繼續優化，
毛利率能再墊高；配合物流逐漸回歸自營管理，費用率亦有望
下降，將進一步提升盈利能力。研究部預估豐祥2027年合併營
收116.46 億元(+23%YOY) ，毛 利 率23.7%(+0.3pct) ，費 用 率
9.1%(-0.3pct)， 預估稅後淨利14.70億元(+23%YOY)， 預估2027
年EPS 21.09元。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下

ChipMOS Technologies 29 September 2026 ab 2
Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation 58.9%
Forecast dividend yield 1.2%
Forecast stock return 60.0%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return 53.8%
Company Description
ChipMOS is a semiconductor back-end supplier with 45-50% of its sales from display driver
IC (~40% from TV and 40% from smartphone and IT and 20% from automotive), 35-40%
from memory (~40% DRAM, ~40% from specialty flash and ~20% from mainstream NAND)

and 10-15% from logic (TV SoC) and mixed signal IC (MEMS, PMIC).
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We have a Buy rating and a NT$166 price target, based on 23x average 2027-28E PE (vs. its
normalized 5-27x PE range). Our target multiple is in line with the average of memory and
driver IC OSAT peers.Downside risks include a weaker-than-expected tech end-market
demand recovery, growing China competition, failure to diversify into the SiPh/optical

component bumping business and a slower ramp of its logic testing business.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the

核心消費 -零售  
Costco Wholesale Corp . (COST )

維持評等  
增加持股(Overweight) 
消費需求仍具韌性，然會員增速逐步常態化  
⚫ FY4Q26 財報表現優於預期，核心 業務穩健成長  
⚫ 會員數續放緩並將成為新常態，美加續卡率逆勢回升  
⚫ FY2027 規劃新增 33 家據點；第三方配送深化全通路布局  
FY4Q26 財報表現優於預期 ，核心營運穩健成長 ：好市多 FY4Q26 營
收 957.2 億美元， 每股盈餘 6.75 美元，皆優於市場預期 。商品淨銷售
額 YoY+ 11. 2%，同店銷售額 (排除油價及匯率影響 )成長 6.7%，來客數
與客單價分別成長 3.3%，顯示成長動能來自客流與消費金額提升，而
非僅依靠價格因素帶動 。管理層指出，美國消費者尚未出現明顯疲弱
跡象，會員在追求日常用品 價格優勢 的同時，仍願意增加旅遊及其他
非必要消費支出，顯示消費行為並非轉趨保守，而是更重視價值感。
獲利方面， 核心毛利率由 供應鏈效率改善、勞動生產力提升帶動，雖
然本季 LIFO (後進先出 )費用增加，但 本業獲利仍維持雙位數成長。  
會員數續放緩並將成為新常態 ，美加續卡率 逆勢回升 ：FY4Q26 會員
費收入 18.5 億美元 (YoY+ 7.3%)，主要受惠於高級會員滲透率提升與
整體會員基數持續擴張。 Costco 會員數擴增 至 8,410 萬(YoY+3.8 %)，
成長持續 放緩，管理層 表示受近年亞洲市場 大規模 展店減少及去年高
基期影響，目前會員增速逐步回歸常態化水準 。具備高消費力的高級
會員數達 4,230 萬人 (YoY+9. 4%)，增速高於整體會員成長，反映會員
結構持續優化 。全球續卡率 89. 8%，美加地區 較上季回升 0.1 個百分
點至 92. 3%。管理層強調，目前更重視會員品質而非會員數量，高階
會員通常具備更高消費額與 高續約率，有助於推 動銷售成長。  
FY202 7 規劃新 增 33 家據點； 推動第三方配送 ：Costco 全球總店數達
到 939 家，FY202 7 規劃新開 33 家據點，其中包含 5 家搬遷店， 朝每
年淨增加 約 30 家據點的長期目標 推進，並預計 投入 75 億美元 資本支
出以支應展店與供應鏈建設。 此外，公司持續深化數位布局，擴大與
Uber Eats 及 DoorDash 合作範圍至全美，管 理層認為第三方配送服務
並非取代實體門市，而是透過提升便利性吸引年輕會員並增加 互動頻
率，進一步強化會員黏著度與消費深度。 附屬業務 (加油站等 )成長優
於整體營收；並透過自有品牌 Kirkland 持續強化會員價值與競爭優勢 。 
評價：Costco 營運具韌性 ，但會員增速趨於常態化，因此略為下調本
益比至 45 倍，以 FY2027 預估 EPS 計算，目標價 1,020 美元，維持增
加持股評等 。風險因子： 會員數和續卡率、同店銷售 、總經環境 。

Document 236: GFHK - CPO _ NPO

Source PDF: all report pdf/GFHK - CPO _ NPO.pdf

### Page 1

Equity Research 
Sector Report | Networking｜Sep 29, 2026 
CPO Accelerates, Yet Stock Implications Vary

Overseas  
Technology

What’s New?  
Following our earlier notes of CPO/NPO Accelerates on Aug  17, we believe that Nvidia’s CPO scale-out switch progress is faster than 
expected. On the other hand, for NPO, due to slower than expected AWS T4 rollout, we revise our total OE shipments forecast to 6.5m/42m 
in 2027E/2028E respectively. We provide our view on recent development and key stocks below. 
Comments  
Nvidia’s Scale-out CPO:  Following Nvidia’s Aug ‑14 announcement, Spectrum ‑X CPO switches have entered mass production. To 
support the ramp, TSMC has expanded CPO testing/inspection capacity, notably adding Insertion 2/3 equipment. Key components such 
as FAU are also progressing well, helped by improving multi ‑plug/unplug yield rates. At the system level, we believe major component 
suppliers are already on site, enabling cleanroom ‑level system integration from early Sep t. We now forecast Nvidia’s CPO switch 
shipments at 15k/16k/30k/100k for 4Q26/1Q27/2Q27/2027. 
Nvidia & AWS Scale-up NPO: Per our AI Platform Value Shift report on Aug 6, NVL576‑based scale‑up CPO/NPO systems are expected 
to begin adoption in 2H27E, with two switch boards per NVSwitch tray, each board carrying four ASICs, paired with four 3.2T OEs/ASIC. 
We reiterate our view that CPO (QM5 + 4 OEs) and NPO (QM5 + 4 OEs on sockets) are progressing in parallel, and our view is similar 
with Street expectations for full NPO adoption at NVL576 launch given NPO maturity. The next to watch is Feynman, which our supply  
chain research indicate is also being developed in both CPO and NPO variants; packaging ‑side visibility suggests CPO (QM6 with six 
6.4T OEs) remains on track. For AWS, given limited near‑term visibility on T4 tape‑out, we now shift our Trainium 4 launch to 1Q28. We 
forecast AWS OE consumption at 0m/12m units for 2H27E/2028E. Incorporating ASIC and AMD volumes, we now expect global scale‑up 
OE demand to reach 6.5m/42m in 2027E/2028E.  
Recommendations   
• Likes: 1) Lumentum (LITE Buy) on CPO acceleration which supports valuation, with CPO/NPO revenue contribution of 12%/26% in 
CY27/CY28; 2) Browave (3163 TT) & Bizlink (3665 TT Buy)  on CPO’s shuffle box of ~8x content vs. NPO ’s, with production to 
accelerate in 1Q27E. 3) Semtech (SMTC Buy) on TIA/Driver for NPO, despite our downward revision, due to tight supply.  
• Dislikes: Largan (3008 TT) as our analysis indicates EPS contribution of NT$20/NT$97 in CY27/CY28, assuming shipments begin 
in 2H27, suggesting implied 20x CY28 valuation which is not particularly cheap, with further risk on market share given the incumbent 
supplier’s ongoing yield improvements and a growing number of new entrants.  
Risks   
1) AI demand slowdown, 2) supply constraint, 3) competition.

Document 241: GLOBAL MARKET CALL_ Bond Vigilantes Gone Wild

Source PDF: all report pdf/GLOBAL MARKET CALL_ Bond Vigilantes Gone Wild.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-04
Pages: 9
